# AGS2 (HUAWEI MediaPad T5, AGS2-L09) device-specific proprietary blobs,
# layered on top of device/huawei/hi6250-common's shared proprietary-files.txt.
# Generated from a static dump of the stock HarmonyOS 2.0 (build 102.0.0.040)
# firmware rather than a live adb pull — paths are verified to exist, but
# file content/hashes have NOT all been verified yet (see README.md). Run
# extract-files.sh against the stock images (or a live device) to pull the
# actual blob content before building.
#
# NOTE: lineage-16.0's extract_utils.sh has no native odm/ partition
# support (that landed in later LineageOS branches). The odm/ entries
# below will extract fine via extract-files.sh, but you'll likely need to
# wire up PRODUCT_COPY_FILES / a dedicated odm Android.mk by hand, or
# backport write_odm_packages from a newer vendor/lineage/build/tools.

# === vendor (no prefix = default /vendor partition) ===
